# Graph Report - ccp-mainbuild-v1  (2026-09-22)

## Corpus Check
- 2783 files · ~6,186,199 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 242 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .csv 187, (none) 17, .xml 10)

## Summary
- 34148 nodes · 78203 edges · 1066 communities (922 shown, 144 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 3292 edges (avg confidence: 0.92)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `884c6e4e`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
